Pls put whom you voted for in past and whom you will vote for in coming Assembly and LS-2019 elections on twitter / FB, as if elections were to happen tomorrow and all parties / groups are contesting. And you may change decision anyday. Pls use #MyPossibleComingVotingPreference and #MyPastVoting
.
And pls dont hold any grudge against anyone voting for anyone --- its their free choice.
.
But at the same time, I would request recallists to focus more on those who have courage to disclose their past / next voting then those who want to keep it confidential.
.
eg my tweets about coming elections are (see links in comment)
.
#MyPossibleComingVotingPreference Loksabha 2019 MyLsConstituency Guj AhmdW008 #Preference1Rrp #Preference2Nota may change
.
#MyPossibleComingVotingPreference Assembly 2017 MyAsmConstituency Ellis041 #Preference1Rrp #Preference2Nota may change
.
#MyPastVoting Loksabha 2014 MyLsConstituency AhmdW08 #Preference1Rrp #Preference2Nota #Pref1DidntContest #VotedForNota

#MyPastVoting Assembly guj 2012 MyAsmConstituency Ellis041 #Preference1Rrp #Preference2Nota #Pref1DidntContest #VotedForNota

The first two twee is about voting in coming Loksabha election. Hashtag used is MyPossibleComingVotingPreference . And #Preference1Rrp #Preference2Nota mean that my first preference is Rrp and if there is no RRP candidate then I will vote for NOTA.
.
And the second and third tweets are about past voting. The hashtag used is #MyPastVoting . And my first preference was RRP and second preference was NOTA. The first preference didnt contest and so I had voted for NOTA and so I used hashtag #VotedForNota.
.
So pls use hashtags #MyPossibleComingVotingPreference and #MyPastVoting and then use appropriate hashtags.
.
I request reach activist / voter to specify who was his first preference in Loksabha 2014 his and who was the second preference in Loksabha-2014. And whether their first preference contested and whom did they voted for,.
.
And pls specify the first and second preferences in coming LS / Assembly elections.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Nov 02 2017 15:45:09 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

if you have more than more than one FB profile, then I request you to put on coverpic a text saying that "this is NOT main profile" , and .....
.
if you have more than more than one FB profile, then I request you to put on coverpic and profilepic a text saying that "this is NOT main profile" and coverpic I request you to put a link to MAIN profile on coverpic in text form.
.
IOW, second / third etc profile MUST clearly say that it is second / third profile, and NOT the main profile.
.
WHY such rule? Why ask ALL to have one and ONLY one profile on FB?
.
Say an activist has 4 profiles, and on one profile, he praises RTR, on second profile he praises SoGa / congress. on third profile he praises NaMo / RSS and on forth profile he praises ArKe / AAP. Then how do I or anyone knows what he stands for? He can tell RRP workers that "see, I am recallist, and other 3 profiles are only to fool congress / rss / aap" workers. And he can tell RSS worker "see, my second profile is main profile, and I am pro-rss and I use other three profiles ONLY too fool recallists and congress/aap workers". And he can go to congress workers and say "see, my 3rd profile is main profile, see I am pro-congress and my other 3 profiles are only to fool recallists and rss / aap workers". And so on.
.
Now FB allows a person to have dozens of profiles. But if we as activists start encouraging people to have multiple profiles, each profile appearing as main profile, then we are allowing people to make different statements to different people.
.
And to ensure that one person is making SAME statements to all persons, it is necessary that we ask ALL to have one profile only.
.
Now many people want 2-3 profiles so that if FB blocks one profile, then they can use other profile. They also use other profile to keep important post as backup. This is fine - because FB does block profiles.
.
So second / third profile is PK. But if you have second / third profile, then the coverpic must clearly say that it is seconddary profile, and coverpic in textform must have link to the main profile.
.
---
.
I request all to have one profile and ensure that if they have second / third profile, then that second / third profile must have coverpic which has link to main profile.
.
I request ALL to discourage others from having more than one profile, and if they have more than one profile, then second profile should clearly mention that it is secondary profile
.
This is necessary to ensure that a person doesnt get chance on FB to say different things to different people.

How to keep FB wall clean - settings
.
(1) Put keep settings so that NO one except you can put any post on your wall. If you like someone's post, then share it. But dont allow anyone except you to put post on your wall
.
(2) Change setting so that a tag will come ONLY after YOU approve. And simply remove all tags !! Share the post if you like, but simply remove tags
.
(3) Every week, delete less important posts. Every month,delete less important posts
.
(4) Make 1-2 album for each IMPORTANT topic and put important posts in those albums only.
.
(5) Use mobile ONLY if you cant afford PC. If you can afford to buy PC at home, then pls do buy one PC and use FB etc from PC as fat as possible
.
(6) If name is in English, then it is easier for others to search. You may keep name in 2 language font. But English name is must for each of search
,
For activists
----------------
.
(7) Never unfriend a person whom your personally know - relatives, school friends, college friends, coilleague from company or ex-company, neighbors etc
.
(8) Unfriend those who personally dont know and post noise.
.
(9) Make index of all important posts related to the causes you support

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Nov 04 2017 13:35:28 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Activists who need mega-funds vs activists who need far LESS funds
.
All activist methods will need some funds -- and some can be self funded i.e. activists are using their own incomes only or depend on contributions of open supporters. I would compare how much funds are needed in different methods for same cause.
.
Activism is generally about improving one or more or all sectors. eg some activists may wish to improve education, some wish to improve health, some would wish to reduce corruption in one or some or all depts, and so on.
.
Now some activist want to improve a sector WITHOUT any law-change as first step while some activists such as recallists FIRST want to bring law-change , and then improve the sector using govt employees, govt contractors and tax money only. IOW, activism of us recallists and many such LEGISLATIVE activist is confined to bringing law-change and then law and govt do rest of the work.
.
I will take "education activists" as example i.e. activists who wish to improve education in India's students.
.
There are several methods proposed by various education activists
.
(a) DIRECT ACTIVISM - Many activists propose methods in which 100s of activists will DIRECTLY teach thousands of kids and improve their education. Some activists take one more step, and are working to setup several Gurukuls or private schools or granted schools to give education to kids. So to teach crores of kids, one would need lakhs of activists working for free or need tens of crores of rupees of donations or govt grants to pay the teachers !!
.
(b) PETITION ACTIVISM - Another set of activists make complaints etc in govt dept, or file petitions / PILs in courts and try to fix the problems.
.
(c) LEGISLATIVE ACTIVISM : some activists such as us recallists confine to raising political campaign to get some laws printed that will improve education. eg Right to Recall District Education Office, RTR State Education Minister, RTR National Education Minister, Saatya System (* not draft, see later) and so on. But we DO NOT believe in doing activist in fixing problems within EXISTING laws, and nor do we suggest that activist should directly teach kids
.
(d) ELECTORAL ACTIVISM -- some activists insist that problem can be reduced merely by replacing CM or PM. So their confine to election campaigning and oppose wasting time in publicizing law-drafts etc. And they do some social work etc, but goal is to collect votes, eg congress / rss / aap workers mainly believe in ELECTORAL ACTIVISM. Their goal is to ensure that their leader becomes PM / CM and nothing else. They do some social work etc. But goal is ONLY to collect votes for their leaders,
.
In (c) i.e. LEGISLATIVE ACTIVISM, we propose to use tax money to pay salary to teachers and there is no donation to pay teachers. The activists' money and cause supporters' money is NOT used to pay teachers,. The money from activists and supporters us used ONLY to publicize ppeffort is ONLY in law-drafts such as RTR-DEO-law-draft , RTR-State-EduMin-law-draft, RTR-National-EduMin-law-draft and so on. And to request activists and voters to send necessary tweets and put necessary posts on FB to publicize the law-drafts using their FB profile and so on
.
In (b) i.e. PETITION ACTIVISM, the activists dont directly teach kids, and they work to improve teaching by filing complains etc in respective dept. So expense is less than direct activism. But finally, taking action on complaints is at discretion of apex GoI officers or judges. So it all boils down what GoI officers and judges will do
.
In (c), there is only information distribution. So there are costs like internet, printing / distributing pamphlets , newspaper advertisements etc. Other than newspaper advt, costs are small. And even if all newspaper advt are included, then also costs PER VOTER is much less than (a). The reason is --- the method (c) only aims at improving efficiency of tax money. and improving efficiency of officers. Neither activists nor contributors are directly paying for education.
.
The ELECTION .ACTIVISM has known problem - the CM / PM often behave like same previous CM / PM.
.
So method (c) - namely LEGISLATIVE activism, is LEAST expensive.
.
But there is also pitfall. (a) will give small output for small input. And so may (b). But in (c),, output is zero till the law-draft comes, no matter how much is input !!
.
So LEGISLATIVE activism has high threshold. And thats one reason why many activists prefer DIRECT activism or PETITION activism or ELECTORAL activism
.
So one cant say that Legislative Activism is "best" - because though it is least expensive, it has high threshold i.e. effects will come only after law-drafts are printed. But Legislative Activism is LEAST expensive of all - because activists and supporters are using their money ONLY to spread information and the physical expenses are done using tax money, and efficiency of tax money increases after laws change.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Nov 05 2017 03:55:26 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Regarding reservation issue
.
(A) Proposed law-drafts to reduce the reservation mess
.
(B) Why both --- those demand reservation and those who oppose reserbation --- are wrong !!!
.
(C) IF CORRUPTION IS REMOVED FROM GOVT JOBS, THEN DEMAND / OPPOSITION FOR RESERVATION WILL BOTH FALL
.
(D) Why I support caste based reservation
.
(E) Why I oppose reservation on economic basis
.
(F) About current demand from some Patels for "Reservation for Patels".
.
(G) Why I oppose demand --- to put Patels in OBC list
.
(H) Electoral angles to current Rservation strife in Patels.
,
(I) why I oppose reservation for women
.
(J) Summary
.
(K) my vidoes on reservation issues
.
===
.
(A) Proposed law-drafts to reduce the reservation mess
.
(A.1) I have proposed a law-draft that will reduce reservation from 49% to 5% of may be less , at cost of Rs 1000 per YEAR per Dalit / ST / OBC i.e. about Rs 84000 crores per year, which will increase existing taxes by about 5% . The draft is at https://www.facebook.com/mehtarahulc/posts/10152992759916922 . If you like the draft , pls follow instructions in the first five comments
.
(A.2) Another proposed law-draft will further reduce caste based reservation by eliminated double options i,e. SC , ST and OBC can opt in General as well as their own quota . The draft is at https://www.facebook.com/mehtarahulc/posts/10152990537221922
.
(A.3) Another proposed law-draft is --- if % of job holders belonging to a sub-caste in a Govt, including its PSUs , is more than their % population of that sub-caste , then reservation for that sub-caste will be suspended for a few years. And Total % reservation will decrease by % population of that caste. The law-draft is at https://www.facebook.com/mehtarahulc/posts/10152993013736922
.
======
.
(B) Why both --- those who demand reservation and those who oppose reservation --- are wrong !!!
.
How many would demand / oppose reservation for job of say sweeper in private companies? Not many. Because they get some Rs 300 a day for work of some Rs 300 a day..
.
How many would demand / oppose reservation for job of say car mechanics? Not many. Because they get Rs 500 to Rs 1000 a day for work of same amount.
.
IOW, where earnings are almost same as labor performed , demand for reservation is low and opposition to reservation in such places is also low.
.
Now why do many want reservation in Govt jobs?
.
The MOST important reason is ---- because Govt jobs fetch huge amount of money for lot less work !! Even when work is high, the money that the govt employees get is far far higher than the work they do. This is in most cases. There may be some exceptions.
.
So basically, IF CORRUPTION IS REMOVED , THEN DEMAND / OPPOSITION FOR RESERVATION WILL BOTH FALL !!!
.
But every time reservation issue comes, I notice that those of 10000s of those who are opposing / supporting reservation , less than 2% are interested in forcing MPs to print law-drafts to reduce corruption. They are ONLY interested in forcing MPs to print law-drafts on reservation issue !!!
.
So as such, both -- those who support reservation and those who oppose it -- are fighting ONLY for "Right to take bribes " !!
.
Those who want reservation are effectively saying --- "Govt employees make Rs X crore of bribes every year ; I am NOT interested in forcing MPs to print law-drafts to reduce corruption. I want to force MPs to print law-drafts that my community should get n% of that Rs X crore, where n% is my % in total population" !!
.
And those who oppose reservation say ""Govt employees make Rs X crore of bribes every year ; I am NOT interested in forcing MPs to print law-drafts to reduce corruption. I want to force MPs to print law-drafts that reservation should end and bribes should go to the most meritorious ones !!".
.
Both statements are ludicrous at best.
.
Take USa for example. USA has de-facto reservation called as Equal Opportunity Employment. This is nothing but some reservation for African Americans etc. There is not much demand for it, and not much opposition either. Because who-so-ever gets benefit of EOE doesnt earn a jackpot, He has to work hard, and gets paid accordingly.
.
Likewise, consider those who sat that reservation is injustice to merit, Well, pls see closing % in medical colleges for SC, ST, OBC, General and self financed quota , and NRI quota. Many times General closing is 92%, Sc will be just 88% i.e. only a few % less, OBC will be just 2% below General, and NRI would be as low as 70% !! IOW, much more damage is being done by self financed quota !!
.
And strangely, those who ippose reservation for SC / ST / OBC on basis of merit never demanded expulsion of Ministers / judges who upheld self-financed quota !!!
..
My point is --- those want to reduce the mess should focus on FORCING MPs to print law-drafts to reduce corruption and reduce management / NRI quota ----- such as RTR , JurySys, narcotest in public in case of high corruption cases etc. When corruption in govt employees reduce, both, demand for reservation will reduce and oppsoition for reservation will reduce.
.
In any case, to reduce the reservation mess, I have proposed 3 law-drafts mentioned in section-A above.
.
====
.
(C) IF CORRUPTION IS REMOVED FROM GOVT JOBS, THEN DEMAND / OPPOSITION FOR RESERVATION WILL BOTH FALL
.
see section-B
.
=====
.
(D) Why I support caste based reservation
,
Caste based discrimination is real. And it is fact that as of now, much of appointments in administration, appointment in courts,, judgements in courts, allocation of contracts, promoion etc go by nepotism. And in such cases, SC , ST and OBC get pushed back despite merit. And so there is a real case of caste based reservation.
.
eg consider High Court judiciary. There are some 600 HCjs, How many are SC / ST and OBC? I request the reader to find out this % himself. Why is number so low? The main reason is --- the decision to appoint Magistrate , sessions judges, HCjs , Scjs etc is all discretion of few judges who in 1950s were all upper caste. And so they exclude out lower castes when appointing next batch. And nepotism is rampant in giving judgements as well.
.
There have been numerous too many cases SC / ST / OBC candidates, who have topped written exam,, literally fial interviews !!! And the main reason isnt lack of merit --- but plain vaninall caste discrimination,
.,
The reason why upper caste Hindus in Congress in late 1940s and early 1950s supported caste based reservation for lower caste was --- they knew that if reservation is not put, then most lower caste would sooner or later fall in the lap of Missionaries. The leaders back then saw no way to reduce nepotism in apex or middle layers. And so they introduced caste based reservation as something that hold SC / ST etc into Hinduism and not walk to Missionaries.
.
The solution to nepotism problem I propose are -- Jury System, RTR , Multi-elections, removing interviws and recruitments by written exams ONLY. We at Right to Recall Group are working to FORCE existing CMs / PM to print these law-drafts. When these law-drafts get printed, one has to see if nepotism has ended. If nepotism ends, there is a case to remove caste based resevation. Till then, I support caste based reservation.
.
=====
.
(E) Why I oppose reservation on economic basis
.
There are people who have lakhs and crores of rupees of cash income and zero income by cheque. They will all manage to get "Below poverty line card" and manage to qualify for Economic Reservation !!! These people have properties in crores, but keep in the name of relatives or keep in cash / gold form.
.
The real poor dont reach class-Xth. So the real poor will never get any reservation benefits anyway. So if N seats are reserved for poor, then all N seats will go "rich in reality , poor on paper" type people.
.
So reservation on economic basis is pointless
.
=====
.
(F) About current demand from some Patels for "Reservation for Patels".
.
The demand is non-starter. The reservation is given only when % of community is X5 and their % in govt jobs is below x%. Patels in Gujarat are about 15% to 20% of population. And their % in Govt jobs is well above 20% . So reservation for Patels is non-stater.
.
The Govt is using lie That reservation cant exceed 49%. Well, Tamil Nadu has been having reservation of 69% since 1980s !!!
.
What SCjs have said is --- Executive can increase reservation in a state above 50% ONLY if 67% of MPs approve. IOW, State Govt can on its own take reservation only to 49%. But if 67% MPs allow , then State Govt can very well take reservation to even 75% or even more !!! (Please google on "Constitution of India , 9th schedule for on this).
.
Besides, SCjs can be also asked to change judgement and can be expelled / replaced.
.
So it is shameful that Govt of Gujarat uses lies like ":reservation cant go beyond 49%" as if it was some law of Physics.
.
But central point is --- % of seats obtained by Patels is well above their % population. So ,even if 15% reservation is kept for Patels, it will make ZERO DIFFERENCE !!
.
=====
.
(G) Why I oppose demand --- to put Patels in OBC list
.
Some Patel leaders have insisted that Patels should be put in OBC list.
.
Not all Patels agree with above proposal.
.
One can make case for 15% EXTRA reservation for Patels. Though it wont make any difference as I explained earlier in above section. But demand to put Patels in OBC list will cause bigger backlash from existing OBCs. OBCs in Gujarat are about 40% of population. They get some 27% reservation. In merit list, they are way behind Patels, on an average, in general. If Patels are put in OBC, then patels will manage to secure at least 20% (my estimate) of seats . So existing OBCs w, who are some 40% of population, will get pushed into some mere 5% to 7% seats.
.
All in all, OBCs dont mind increasing reservation to 65% and giving next 16% to Patels. But giving slice of OBCs's pie to Patels will be seriously non-acceptable to them
,
I oppose this demand anyway
.
====
.
(H) Electoral angles to current Rservation strife in Patels
.
From 1977 to 1995 , Congress supported reservation. Whenever there was opposition to reservation, BJP created an image that they are anti-reservation !! BJP-leaders never made any statement against reservation. But they did create an image that the are against reservation !! So many Patels and other Upper Caste Hindus became supporters of BJP with hope that BJP will remove reservation when BJP comes into power. Now bwteen , 1984 to 2005 , BJP used Hinduvaad to get votes from lower caste Hindus. Hinduvaad was used by Ram Janam Bhoomi , Krishna Janam Bhoomi, Kashi Vishvanath, art-370, Unifrm Civil Code, 2002 riots etc. But Hinduvaad started waning by 2005. So BJP was left with no option but to support SC , ST, OBC when it came to jobs,college seats, govt contracts etc.
.
This has created feeling of "being backstabbed" in Patels. Many Patels feel that BJP leaders have backstabbed them or cheated them.
.
This coupled with unemployment and under-employment, and over expectations about employment , has made many upper caste youth restless. They have been made to believe that reservation will reduce unemployment and under-employment !!
.
So feeling of being cheated by BJP , unemployment, under-employment etc are reasons why volcanoes has re-erupted.
.
I have proposed some law-drafts to reduce the reservation mess in section-A
.
The law-drafts to reduce unemployment are --- (a) wealth tax and removal of GST / VAT / CST / service tax / excise so that land prices fall and more factories start (b) Jury System so that corruption and harassment of Govt officers on factory owners reduce (c) Jury System over labor disputes (d) DDMCRAM -- so that demand increase and so forth, I have explained these proposed law-drtafts in chap-63 of my book -- see link in 1st comment
.
As per final electoral outcomes, whether it will make Hardik Patel form new Politucal Party or not, whether Patels will vote for that party or BJP ---- I really dont bother myself with that, My goal is to get some law-drafts printed in Gazette of India --- such as TCP-law-draft , DDMRCAM-law-draft, RTR-law-drafts, JurySys-law-drafts, wealth tax law-drafts etc etc. I dont bother wuith who will get how many votes.
.
====
.
(I) why I oppose reservation for women
.
It is unrelated to this existing reservation crisis. So I will be brief. I oppose reservation for women, Because women were never discriminated at the time of giving job or education opportunity.
.
====
.
(J) Summary
,
All in all, my request to ALL true non-80G-activists is to focus on law-drafts to reduce reservation mess in section-A, and law-drafts to reduce unemployment mentioned in chap-63 . That should reduce several problems , including discrimination and reservation.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Nov 05 2017 09:28:40 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Competition in causes (and proposed law-drafts) such as
.
(a) one may feel is his cause is done first, then other’s cause will automatically get done afterwards or will get done easily".
.
(b) one may feel that other cause is good, but only after his own cause is first implemented , and if other cause is implemented before his own cause, then there it will harm India !!
.
and many more valid or “valid looking” reasons.
.
----
.
(1) Two activists may be opposing each other
.
Many times, activists will be opposed to each other. eg many activists’ cause is to have strictest implementation of anti-opium law i.e. they want a legal / police system where ALL opium users and sellers would get long prison sentences asap. Whereas, I want complete legalization of opium. And the law-draft I have proposed, namely "Jury Trials in cases of opium growing, selling, buying, reselling and using" is more likely to legalize opium. So clearly, I and anti-opium activists can work together on this issue. IOW, there are many cases where activists are opposed to one another.
.
---
.
(2) Agreeing but still poles apart – Reasons !!
.
But there are cases where activists agree on causes, or “seem to agree” on causes. But disagree completely when it comes to campaign !!
.
-------
.
(2.1) Reason-1 – My cause should be kept first !!
.
And they may have valid reasons why their cause should be first. I will discuss some reasons I got.
.
.
Now in my activism method, given any cause, I either oppose a cause or I have given law-drafts that imo implements the cause. So for some 20 causes, there are some 100 law-drafts I have proposed. And for 100 causes there are some 300 law-drafts that I have proposed.
.
And activism method I propose to other activists is – he can publicize the drafts he supports on twitter, FB, pamphlets, newspaper advt, election contesting, election campaigning etc. And he may ignore the drafts that he things are not useful, and he may ignore the drafts / causes he isnt interested in or opposes.
.
Now some law-drafts are far important to me than others. eg DDMRCAM (aka MRCM) is most important to me. But to fellow activists, I never insist that they should promote DDMRCAM more than other law-drafts. Each activist decides which law-drafts he wishes to publicize
.
----
.
(2.2) Reason-2 ---– my cause or my proposed law-draft is more fundamental
.
But many activists insist that law-drafts that they propose are “more important” and “more fundamental” then others’ law-drafts.
.
And there is one more situation --- many activists insist that law-draft-B is good, but must be passed ONLY after cause-A has been fulfilled !! eg one statement I get is “RTR-law-drafts are good, but RTR-law-drafts need educated citizens and so RTR-law-drafts should be passed ONLY after education casue has been fulfilled and till then RTR-law-drafts should NOT be passed !!” IOW, not only his cause of education is ahead of RTR, but till his education cause has been fulfilled, RTR should be opposed !!!
.
In my campaign method, all this doesn’t exist. If I support a law-draft-B, I never put condition that law-draft-A must be passed BEFORE or cause-A must be fulfilled before and so on. IMO, if an activist likes law-draft-A, then he need not wait till casue-B is met or law-draft-B is passed. Each law-draft can do good on its own.
.
---
.
(2.3) My law-draft / cause subsumed your law-draft / cause !!
.
Say activist-A supports cause-A and law-draft-A and activist-B supports cause-B and law-draft-B. Now activist-A may make statement that “I support law-draft-B, but activism on law-draft-B is not needed !! Because once law-draft-A is passed, law-draft-B will NOT be needed or law-draft-B will get passed without activism !!” IOW, as per activist-A, activist-B “should” end his activism on law-draft-B, and should focus only on activism for law-draft-A.
.
The argument of subsuming may or may not be correct.
.
Eg1. consider law-draft-A that “all landownership records should be made public”. And consider law-draft-B that “land ownership of trusts should be made public”. The law-draft-A covers law-draft-B, but from activism point of view – it does not. Because there can be many pro-privacy activists who will support law-draft-B and NOT law-draft-A !! So getting law-draft-A printed is more difficult, and so some activist may campaign for law-draft-B. So activist-B has valid reason not to agree with activist-A.
.
Eg2. consider law-draft-A which reduces poverty and law-draft-B which improve ration card system. Activist-A may feel that “once poverty reduces, ration card system isnt needed and so activism for law-draft-B isnt needed” !! But activist-B may feel different. Because getting law-draft-A passed may take longer, and even if it passes, there is some possibility that poverty may not reduce for months. And above all, person who wants to improve ration card system may not have so much faith in activist-A that he will agree to spend time in explaining law-draft-A to 1000s of supporters of law-draft-B !! So even if law-draft-A may be subsuming law-draft-B, the activism for law-draft-A imo does NOT subsume activism for law-draft-B. The activist-A and activist-B both have valid reason for not working on same campaign steps.
.
---
.
(3) Proposed solution
.
The two solutions I propose are
.
(3a) that each activist-A should put a status post in his FB profile which explains steps he wants other activists to take. And the status should be kept in a way that activist-B who is visiting profile of activist-A can reach by following links from his cover page. Eg the steps I ask other activists to take are at - fb.com/mehtarahulc/posts/10154841368981922 . The link is in coverpage description. Other activist can read the writeup and decide how much to follow, and how much not to.
.
(3b) Each activists should make list of law-drafts he supports (and/or causes) in his FB statuses, and these statuses should be reachable from his FB cover picture. And every other activist can take a look, and work on law-drafts A is proposing or ignore his law-drafts. i.e. no pressing need for a common minimum program or common maximum program.
.
----
.
(4) Conclusion
.
My point here is --- when activists oppose each other’s cause, disagreement is visible.
.
But many times, when activist-A supports cause-B and viceversa, then it would NOT mean that they can agree on one campaign method. And some of the reasons can be
.
(4a) activist-A feels that if cause-B or law-draft-B is implemented BEFORE law-draft-A or cause-A, then it will cause harm !! And so cause-B or law-draft-B, is good, but should be postponed till cause-A or law-draft-A is implemented
.
(4b) activist-A feels that if law-draft-A is implemented, then cause-B gets fulfilled or will get fulfilled easily, whereas activist-B doesn’t think same or doesn’t want to spend time in explaining law-draft-A to 100 other activists working on law-draft-B implemented
.
And many more valid or valid looking reasons !!
.
And solution I propose is – each activist can put steps he proposes other activists to take in an FB status and he can put law-drafts he supports in FB statuses. And the statuses should be reachable by links starting from cover picture. That way, one activist can decide how many of law-drafts given by other activist does he wish to support / oppose / ignore.

Many activists may be interested only in creating an asset-rich NGO and interested in any national cause AFTER THAT (or may be never) !!
.
I came across many "education activists" (i.e. activists working to improve education in poor kids across India) , goseva-activists (protect cows across India), health activists (i.e. those who wish to improve health of citizens of India) etc etc
.
Now over 90% of SENIOR education activists refused to support Right to Recall District Education Law-draft on their twitter / FB walls. And they also refused to support RTR-State-Education-Minister law-draft and RTR-Central-Education-Minister law-draft on their FB/twitter walls !! And they did NOT propose any law-draft to improve education across state / India.
.
And over 90% of SENIOR gosevaks refused to support "narcotest in gohatya cases" law-draft or "Jury Trial in gohatya cases" law-draft or "right to recall over state cow welfare minister" etc on thwir twitter / FB wall. Nor did they propose any law-drafts that can reduce cow slaughter across state / India.
.
And same way, over 90% of SENIOR health activists , i.e. who wish to improve health refused to support Right to Recall over District Food Anti-Adulteration Officer, RTR State Health Minister etc etc.
.
Some of their JUNIORS do support these law-drafts,. But as is case with many NGOs etc, the juniors depend on approvals of the seniors
.
The reasons can be following
.
(1) NGO owner doesnt see any need to change laws. Or doesnt believe that activists and activism can do any law-change.
.
(2) NGO-owners may be interested in FIRST building a huge NGO, and then only after he wishes to serve the cause at state / national level !! So he first wants his NGO to have a few crores of assets or a few 10s of crores of assets, and then only AFTER that he wishes to serve the cause at state / national level.
.
(3) And once he meets the goal of having an NGO of say assets worth Rs 10 crores, he may decide to serve the cause ONLY after NGO has asset of Rs 100 crores and so on. i.e. he may never be able to decide to work for the cause at state / national levels !!
.
So there are many valid or "valid looking" reasons.
.
So my request to junior activists is -- if YOU wish to spend time and energy in bringing changes in laws in India, chances are high that your senior NGO-owner or party-owner will ask you to work on law-change AFTER his NGO / party has acquired assets worth crores !! So you need to decide whether you wish to wait or not. My suggestion will be --- do NOT wait for his NGO to acquire assets of crores ; pls start supporting drafts on FB / twitter / pamphlets etc asap
.
----
.
(Aside :
.
In RTR-movement, we never ask volunteers to donate or get donations except for newspaper advt.
.
So in newspaper advt, the senior will NOT be accumulating any material assets in his name or in name of party or any NGO. So one may say -- the senior activist will get some fame. Well, he may, get fame. But primary fame goes to the law-draft and the senior recallists ONLY gets secondary fame. But compare this to other NGOs. Here the NGO-owner converts donation into some land / building etc owned by that NGO and the NGO-owner also gets primary fame !! IOW , donations to most NGOs add assets and fame to the NGO-owners whereas advertisement contributions in Right to Recall only add "fame" to the law-drafts and ONLY some fame to senior recallists and zero assets to senior recallsts or RRP. So this is an important difference when it comes fund gathering in RRP and others)

(a) MINUS 1000 sqft of land per family
(b) minus 2000 sqft of construction per family
(c) 1 acre of agricultural land per PERSON, so 5 acre per family
(d) income tax paid
(e) electricity and fuel taxes paid
(f) minus 15% of salaries paid to employees
(g) minus Rs 200 per year per follower of religious or charitable trust.
.
And land of all trusts including religious trusts including temples, mosques, churches, gurudwara etc will also be taxed by same law. So if a person is using his land / office / flats for business or true charitable purposes, then he will be paying income tax or salaries or tax on electricity or will have followers. So defacto wealth tax on him will be almost zero. But if he has hoarded huge land, then he will end up paying huge wealth tax. And so he will be forced to sell / rent land and so land prices will fall. Imo, once this law is printed, the land prices in cities will fall to 1/10th of present value !! Yes, 1/10th !! i.e. imo a plot which is today (oct-2017) costing Rs 100,000 per sqmt will cost no more than Rs 10000 per sqmt !! And so house prices will fall, rents wll fall. Bank Transaction Tax hoax
.
BTT people will show you RBI data on cheque and epayments. It is Rs 15 lak crore per DAY. So they say
.
GoI needs Rs 30 crore as expense (center / state / local)
.
Total bank transactions = Rs 15 lakh crore per day * 365 = about 5400 lakh crore per year
.
So transaction tax rate needed = 30 / 5400 = about 0.5%
.
The above calculations IGNORE the facts that
.
(1) Many transactions are bank to bank
.
(2) Many transactions are govt to govt
.
(3) many transactions are when person is paying tax to GoI or collecting refunds
,
(4) many transactions are PERSON to SAME PERSON i.e. I transfer money from my Bank of India main account to SBI once every 2 months.
.
(5) Many transactions are between family members
.
(6) Many transactions are share market and bond market transactions eg person buys commercial papers and then paper gets repaid in 7 to 30 days
.
The real transactions, in which material or land is moving - is only a small fraction.
.
BTT taxes real activity such as manufacturing and levies zero tax on land hoarding !! So much for argument that BTT will promote industries !!
.
Anil Bokiiji started BTT proposal in 1990s. It has been over 20 years. And he still has NOT given any DRAFT .
.
BTT is a nonsense proposal
.
Their goal is to block activists from taking interest in REAL proposals like Wealth tax so that proposals like GST can move ahead
.
---
.
Essentially, there are financial transactions and non financial transactions. Financial transactions are where money is moving, but there is no material moving below. And matrial or non-financial transactions are when material or land is moving along with money. eg say X buys vegetables for Rs 100 and so some material moved. But say Central Govt paid Rs 1000 crore to some State Govt. Then material moved = 0. A bordeline case is interest on loans - X loans Rs 1 lakh to Y and Y gives Rs 1.03 lakh after 1 month, where Rs 3000 was interest. Here material moved = 0, money moved = Rs 2.03 lakhs. But there was risk - and so one can take Rs 3000 as cost of risk or value of some material wealth generated from loan.

(B) Why both --- those who demand reservation and those who oppose reservation --- are wrong !!!
.
How many would demand / oppose reservation for job of say sweeper in private companies? Not many. Because they get some Rs 300 a day for work of some Rs 300 a day..
.
How many would demand / oppose reservation for job of say car mechanics? Not many. Because they get Rs 500 to Rs 1000 a day for work of same amount.
.
IOW, where earnings are almost same as labor performed , demand for reservation is low and opposition to reservation in such places is also low.
.
Now why do many want reservation in Govt jobs?
.
The MOST important reason is ---- because Govt jobs fetch huge amount of money for lot less work !! Even when work is high, the money that the govt employees get is far far higher than the work they do. This is in most cases. There may be some exceptions.
.
So basically, IF CORRUPTION IS REMOVED , THEN DEMAND / OPPOSITION FOR RESERVATION WILL BOTH FALL !!!
.
But every time reservation issue comes, I notice that those of 10000s of those who are opposing / supporting reservation , less than 2% are interested in forcing MPs to print law-drafts to reduce corruption. They are ONLY interested in forcing MPs to print law-drafts on reservation issue !!!
.
So as such, both -- those who support reservation and those who oppose it -- are fighting ONLY for "Right to take bribes " !!
.
Those who want reservation are effectively saying --- "Govt employees make Rs X crore of bribes every year ; I am NOT interested in forcing MPs to print law-drafts to reduce corruption. I want to force MPs to print law-drafts that my community should get n% of that Rs X crore, where n% is my % in total population" !!
.
And those who oppose reservation say ""Govt employees make Rs X crore of bribes every year ; I am NOT interested in forcing MPs to print law-drafts to reduce corruption. I want to force MPs to print law-drafts that reservation should end and bribes should go to the most meritorious ones !!".
.
Both statements are ludicrous at best.
.
Take USa for example. USA has de-facto reservation called as Equal Opportunity Employment. This is nothing but some reservation for African Americans etc. There is not much demand for it, and not much opposition either. Because who-so-ever gets benefit of EOE doesnt earn a jackpot, He has to work hard, and gets paid accordingly.
.
Likewise, consider those who sat that reservation is injustice to merit, Well, pls see closing % in medical colleges for SC, ST, OBC, General and self financed quota , and NRI quota. Many times General closing is 92%, Sc will be just 88% i.e. only a few % less, OBC will be just 2% below General, and NRI would be as low as 70% !! IOW, much more damage is being done by self financed quota !!
.
And strangely, those who ippose reservation for SC / ST / OBC on basis of merit never demanded expulsion of Ministers / judges who upheld self-financed quota !!!
..
My point is --- those want to reduce the mess should focus on FORCING MPs to print law-drafts to reduce corruption and reduce management / NRI quota ----- such as RTR , JurySys, narcotest in public in case of high corruption cases etc. When corruption in govt employees reduce, both, demand for reservation will reduce and oppsoition for reservation will reduce.

(D) Why I support caste based reservation
,
Caste based discrimination is real. And it is fact that as of now, much of appointments in administration, appointment in courts,, judgements in courts, allocation of contracts, promoion etc go by nepotism. And in such cases, SC , ST and OBC get pushed back despite merit. And so there is a real case of caste based reservation.
.
eg consider High Court judiciary. There are some 600 HCjs, How many are SC / ST and OBC? I request the reader to find out this % himself. Why is number so low? The main reason is --- the decision to appoint Magistrate , sessions judges, HCjs , Scjs etc is all discretion of few judges who in 1950s were all upper caste. And so they exclude out lower castes when appointing next batch. And nepotism is rampant in giving judgements as well.
.
There have been numerous too many cases SC / ST / OBC candidates, who have topped written exam,, literally fial interviews !!! And the main reason isnt lack of merit --- but plain vaninall caste discrimination,
.,
The reason why upper caste Hindus in Congress in late 1940s and early 1950s supported caste based reservation for lower caste was --- they knew that if reservation is not put, then most lower caste would sooner or later fall in the lap of Missionaries. The leaders back then saw no way to reduce nepotism in apex or middle layers. And so they introduced caste based reservation as something that hold SC / ST etc into Hinduism and not walk to Missionaries.
.
The solution to nepotism problem I propose are -- Jury System, RTR , Multi-elections, removing interviws and recruitments by written exams ONLY. We at Right to Recall Group are working to FORCE existing CMs / PM to print these law-drafts. When these law-drafts get printed, one has to see if nepotism has ended. If nepotism ends, there is a case to remove caste based resevation. Till then, I support caste based reservation.
.

(E) Why I oppose reservation on economic basis
.
There are people who have lakhs and crores of rupees of cash income and zero income by cheque. They will all manage to get "Below poverty line card" and manage to qualify for Economic Reservation !!! These people have properties in crores, but keep in the name of relatives or keep in cash / gold form.
.

.
So reservation on economic basis is pointless
.

(F) About current demand from some Patels for "Reservation for Patels".
.
The demand is non-starter. The reservation is given only when % of community is X5 and their % in govt jobs is below x%. Patels in Gujarat are about 15% to 20% of population. And their % in Govt jobs is well above 20% . So reservation for Patels is non-stater.
.
The Govt is using lie That reservation cant exceed 49%. Well, Tamil Nadu has been having reservation of 69% since 1980s !!!
.
What SCjs have said is --- Executive can increase reservation in a state above 50% ONLY if 67% of MPs approve. IOW, State Govt can on its own take reservation only to 49%. But if 67% MPs allow , then State Govt can very well take reservation to even 75% or even more !!! (Please google on "Constitution of India , 9th schedule for on this).
.
Besides, SCjs can be also asked to change judgement and can be expelled / replaced.
.
So it is shameful that Govt of Gujarat uses lies like ":reservation cant go beyond 49%" as if it was some law of Physics.
.
But central point is --- % of seats obtained by Patels is well above their % population. So ,even if 15% reservation is kept for Patels, it will make ZERO DIFFERENCE !!
.

(G) Why I oppose demand --- to put Patels in OBC list
.
Some Patel leaders have insisted that Patels should be put in OBC list.
.
Not all Patels agree with above proposal.
.

But demand to put Patels in OBC list will cause bigger backlash from existing OBCs. OBCs in Gujarat are about 40% of population. They get some 27% reservation. In merit list, they are way behind Patels, on an average, in general. If Patels are put in OBC, then patels will manage to secure at least 20% (my estimate) of seats . So existing OBCs w, who are some 40% of population, will get pushed into some mere 5% to 7% seats.
.
All in all, OBCs dont mind increasing reservation to 65% and giving next 16% to Patels. But giving slice of OBCs's pie to Patels will be seriously non-acceptable to them
,
I oppose this demand anyway
.

(H) Electoral angles to current Rservation strife in Patels
.
From 1977 to 1995 , Congress supported reservation. Whenever there was opposition to reservation, BJP created an image that they are anti-reservation !! So many Patels and other Upper Caste Hindus became supporters of BJP with hope that BJP will remove reservation when BJP comes into power. .
This coupled with unemployment and under-employment, and over expectations about employment , has made many upper caste youth restless. They have been made to believe that reservation will reduce unemployment and under-employment !!

Aadhar --- I support aadhar system except that GoI should remove all US companies in the system.
.
And my proposal is - OpenAdhaar i.e. to make it completely open i.e. anyone in world can buy / download DVD with aadhar#, name, pic (of males only, and pic of male relative in case of females), picode (not full address), city name, finger print, NO RETINA SCAN and names/aadhar# of ALL close relatives.
.
So in my proposal, aadhar card owner will have NO choice --- this info will become public.And females can put their pic if they want, but for males there will be NO choice.
.
In addition, ONLY if he wants, then the public database will have his contact number, his FB id etc
.
---
.
I propose to get this law passed using TCP (a referendum like system). So if say 45 crore voters approve, then PM will decide. Or, if and when 50% loksabha MPs decide. So those who oppose this system have several ways -- they can convince PM not to print such law, they can ask voters NOT to register YES on this proposal and they can ask MPs not to vote YES on this proposal.
.
But if against all odds, if PM prints this law or if MPs print this law and if 45 crore voters dont oppose it or rather support it, then those who dont like this law will have to live with this law.
.
I will soon upload OpenAadhar proposal on newindia dot in.
.
-------------
.
Digital payment frauds are because banks are giving ATM card which come with (a) physical retail payment option and
(b) net payment option with OTP, and also
(c) come with no OTP international net payment option
.
The customer has no way to specify that I want ONLY ATM or I ONLY want ATM plus net physical retail (i.e.goods can be sold ONLY to card owner in person and not on net). And he cant even disable international non OTP payments !!
.
And worse, benks start net baking without asking !!
.
I got letter from bank that net banking has been enabled on my account and I had to personally go and give letter that I dont want it !!
.
So problem is with debit / credit card system. Not with Aadhar,. Now of course, aadhar linking is making digital paynents frauds worse,. But solution is to enable customer to have
.
(a) ATM only card
(b) ATM + physical retail ONLY card
(c) ATM + physical retail + OTP based india only net banking card
(d) international transaction credit card (NO DEBITcard option)
(e) credit card must have 2% charge to cover frauds only
(f) credit card company and NOT card owner should be asked to file FIR
.
Above will solve the digital payment fraud mess. I see no need to remove aadhar system

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Nov 08 2017 06:55:56 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Voting for a proposed law-draft on PM's website newindia.in and sharing your support on FB increases the POSSIBILITY that the law-draft will get passed. There is no guarantee
.
And for that matter, NO amount of campaigning gurantees that a law will get passed with 100% guarantee
.
Each step increases probability
.
eg say
.
(1) you vote on newindia.in/causes/DdMrcam - possibility that DdMrcam will pass increases
.
(2) you share your vote casting on FB. The possibility that DdMrtcam will pass will further increase
.
(3) you share your vote casting on twitter. Then possibility that DdMrtcam will pass will further increase
.
(4) You add more description on FB when you share vote casting on FB. Possibility increases
.
(5) You make ONE FB album which has link to ALL vote casting FB posts and tweets --- so any user who comes to that album will NOT see all posts but see ONLY posts related to votes !! Then possibility that ;laws will pass further inccreases !!
.
(6) You unfollow congress / rss / aap etc activists who refuse to support these laws on newindia.in, FB and tweeter --- then possibility that these laws will further increase !!
.
(7) You work for election campaign for those who are publicizing DdMrcam and other such laws you support,. Then possibility that these laws will pass further increases
.
So each step increases possibility. And as more people take these steps, possibility further increases
.
And NOTHING takes possibility to 100% !! Nothing gives 100% guarantee. If one says that --- take these and these and these campaign steps, then possibility that a law will get passed becomes 100%, then I dismiss that statement as errorneous
.
Finally, newindia.in gives ONLY voter count. It does NOT give names, ph# of addresses. It allows login using email and so it allows fake voting. We should ask PM to make sure that voting can be done ONLY via accounts which have mobile which are linked with aadhar. That will reduce spurious voting.
.
Bit even with fake voting, newindia helps in publicizing the law-drafts amongst friends YOU personally know. And while votecount can be useless, by knowing that a person DID NOT vote for this cause, you know that that person is NOT committed to the cause.
.
Now votecount can be fake, but "NOT VOTING" also provides useful information about lack of commitment in a given activist.
.
So, pls do vote for causes you support on newindia dot in. And pls share that on FB / twitter

कई रिकालिस्ट्स की आर्थिक स्थिति इतनी माकूल है कि उन्हें प्रचार करने या चुनाव लड़ने के लिए आर्थिक सहयोग की जरूरत नहीं रहती है। किन्तु कतिपय कारणों के चलते इनमे ज्यादातर रिकालिस्ट्स चुनाव लड़ने में रुचि नहीं दिखाते। और कुछ कार्यकर्ता ऐसे भी है जो इन कानूनों पर चुनाव लड़ते है, किन्तु उनकी आर्थिक स्थिति उतनी बेहतर नहीं है।उदाहरण के लिए, मेरी जानकारी में ऐसे कार्यकर्ता है जो 20 हजार माहवार वेतन पर काम करते हुए 4 सदस्यीय परिवार का पालन करते है एवं चुनाव लड़ने पर अपना समय तथा धन भी खर्च करते है।
.
तो जो रिकालिस्ट्स समर्थ है उन्हें ऐसे प्रत्याशियों को आर्थिक सहयोग करना चाहिए जो चुनावों में भाग ले रहे है।
.
----
.
कृपया अपनी फेसबुक प्रोफाइल पर एक एल्बम बनाए और जब भी आप किसी चुनावी कार्यकर्ता को आर्थिक सहयोग दें तो इस राशी को इस एल्बम पोस्ट में दर्ज करें, ताकि रिकॉर्ड के लिए इस अंशदान के अभिलेख आसानी से जुटाए जा सके।

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Thu Nov 09 2017 09:07:58 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

पद्मावती ; फिल्म का प्रचार करने के नाम पर चलाये जा रहे फर्जी समाधान Vs वास्तविक समाधान
.
(1) फर्जी समाधान -- फेसबुक प्रोफाइल पर इसके विरोध में पोस्ट लिखें , फिल्म के खिलाफ नारेबाजी करें , इसके अभिनेताओं एवं निर्देशकों को गालियां दें, थियेटर मालिकों को धमकाए, उनके साथ मारपीट करें, तोड़फोड़ करें एवं इस फिल्म के खिलाफ ट्वीटर पर अभियान चलाएं। इन सब हरकतों से इस फिल्म का प्रचार होगा और जितना ही इसका विरोध होगा उतना ही अंतराष्ट्रीय बाजार में इसका मूल्य बढ़ता जाएगा। इस प्रकार के विरोध एवं प्रचार से इस फिल्म के सेटेलाइट अधिकारों के दाम भी उछल जाएंगे और निर्माता को मोटी कमाई होगी।
.
सरकार से इसे बेन करने की मांग करना भी व्यर्थ है। अव्वल तो सरकार इसे बेन नहीं करेगी। और यदि कर भी देती है तो निर्माता शेष विश्व में इसे रिलीज करके मोटा मुनाफा कमा सकेगा। हिन्दू रानी का विद्रूप चित्रण करने के आरोप में बेन करने के एवज में सऊदी अरेबिया जैसे देश इसे टेक्स फ्री भी कर सकते है या दर्शको को इसे देखने के लिए उलटे पैसे भी दे सकते है। इसके अलावा मिशनरीज तो है ही।
.
तो आप देखेंगे कि पेड मीडिया इस फिल्म के विरोध को पर्याप्त हवा दे रहा है, विरोध की खबरें दिखाई जा रही है तथा विभिन्न संगठन खुद का एवं फिल्म का प्रचार करने के लिए इस फिल्म का जमकर विरोध कर रहे है। कई कार्यकर्ता भी इन सबकी मिली जुली रणनीति के शिकार होकर जाने अनजाने इस फिल्म का प्रचार करने में लगे है।
.
1.a) इससे पहले पीके, ओह माय गॉड , दंगल जैसी फिल्मो का विरोध हो चुका है, और ये फिल्मे रिलीज भी हुयी और बेहद सफल भी हुयी। पद्मावती का फर्जी विरोध करने वाले जानते है कि इस विरोध से जो भी होगा वह इसका प्रचार होगा। अंततोगत्वा फिल्म रिलीज होगी और देश भर के सिनेमाघरों में चलेगी। यदि फिल्म की ओपनिंग कमजोर रहती है तो कुछ हुड़दंगी संगठन थियेटर में जाकर तोड़फोड़ कर देंगे या निर्देशक के पुतले जला देंगे जिससे इसका प्रचार होगा और शेष राज्यों में यह फिल्म भीड़ खींचने लगेगी !!!
.
1.b) रिलीज होने के बाद इस फिल्म के सेटेलाइट अधिकार बेचे जायेगे और पीके , ओह माय गॉड , दंगल की तरह इस फिल्म को प्रति सप्ताह तब तक दिखाया जायेगा जब तक कि प्रत्येक भारतीय इस फिल्म को न देख ले।
.
1.c) सरकार इस फिल्म को बेन नहीं करना चाहती है। किन्तु यदि बेन करने का पर्याप्त दबाव बन जाता है तो वे इसे बेन कर देंगे और निर्माता से कहेंगे कि कोर्ट से इसकी एन ओ सी ले आये। निर्माता 2 दिन में कोर्ट से रिलीज का आदेश ले आएगा। कोर्ट सरकार को यह भी आदेश देगी कि इस फिल्म के प्रसारण की समुचित सुरक्षा के प्रबंध करें। कोर्ट के आदेश पर जब पुलिस थियेटरो के बाहर खड़ी रहेगी तो तोड़फोड़ करने की मंशा रखने वाले संगठन खामोश हो कर घर चले जाएंगे। जजों का पुतला फूंकने में जो साहस चाहिए वे उससे वंचित है।
.
1.d) दरअसल फिल्म से मुनाफा कमाना कोई विषय ही नहीं है। फिल्म में पैसा सऊदी अरेबिया / मिशनरीज ने लगाया है और उन्हें इससे मुनाफा कमाने की कोई फ़िक्र भी नहीं है। वे हिन्दू चरित्रों का नकारात्मक चित्रण करना चाहते है। वे यह सन्देश देना चाहते है कि जौहर करना एक अतार्किक एवं बेवकूफी भरा कदम था, और पद्मावती को खिलजी के साथ चले जाना चाहिए था। मदर इण्डिया ऑस्कर के अंतिम दौर से इसीलिए बाहर हो गयी थी क्योंकि "उनका" मानना था कि नरगिस को साहूकार से आर्थिक मदद लेने के बदले अपनी देह का सौदा कर लेना चाहिए था। मददगार को अपनी देह न सौंप कर नायिका ने बच्चों के पालन पोषण से समझौता किया था।
.
---------
.
(2) वास्तविक समाधान
.
@pmoindia पर ट्वीट करें कि फिल्म के प्रसारण का फैसला करने के लिए ज्यूरी ट्रायल किया जाए।
.
ज्यूरी मंडल का गठन :
.
१) ज्यूरी में 500 सदस्य होंगे। इसमें आधे सदस्य मेवाड़ प्रदेश से तथा शेष सदस्य राजस्थान से होंगे।
.
२) ज्यूरी सदस्यों का आयु वर्ग 25 से 55 वर्ष के बीच होगा।
.
३) इन सदस्यों का चयन प्रदेश की मतदाता सूचियों से अक्रमत ( रेंडमली ) विधि से किया जाएगा।
.
४) ज्यूरी नियमति रूप से सुनवाई करेगी और तीन चौथाई बहुमत से आरोपियों का नार्को टेस्ट ले सकेगी।
.
५) ज्यूरी जो भी फैसला दे उसकी अपील राष्ट्रीय ज्यूरी में की जा सकती है। राष्ट्रीय ज्यूरी में 1500 सदस्य होंगे और इनका चयन पूरे देश से किया जाएगा।
.
६) राष्ट्रीय ज्यूरी के फैसले की अपील सुप्रीम कोर्ट में नहीं की जा सकेगी न ही संसद इस फैसले की खिलाफ कोई प्रस्ताव ला सकती है। किन्तु इस फैसले को राष्ट्रिय स्तर पर जनमत संग्रह की किसी प्रक्रिया द्वारा तब पलटा जा सकेगा जब देश के 51% मतदाता ऐसा समर्थन दर्शाएं।
.
------
.
इस ज्यूरी के सामने फिल्म के निर्माता निर्देशक एवं विभिन्न अन्य पक्षकार फिल्म के बारे में अपना स्पष्टीकरण या पक्ष रख सकते है। ज्यूरी सदस्य फिल्म देखकर यह तय कर सकते कि फिल्म को बेन करने के अलावा राष्ट्रिय चरित्रों का विद्रूप चित्रण करने के आरोप में निर्माता , निर्देशक एवं अभिनेताओ को क्या सजा दी जानी चाहिए। यदि मुझे ज्यूरी में आने का अवसर मिलता है और यह पाया जाता है कि फिल्म में विद्रूप चित्रण किया गया है तो मेरा वोट इन्हें जेल में भेजने के लिए होगा। निर्माता , निर्देशक, एवं फिल्म के तीनो मुख्य अभिनेताओं / अभिनेत्री को कम से कम 3 साल की सजा दी जानी चाहिए। तीन साल की यह सजा इन्हें चित्तोड़ जेल में काटनी होगी और इन्हें एक भी दिन के लिए पैरोल पर रिहा नहीं किया जाएगा।
.
ऐसे फैलसे से यह फिल्म देश के अलावा अन्य देशो में भी ऑफिसियली रिलीज नहीं हो पाएगी। अलबत्ता टोरेंट पर यह फिल्म उपलब्ध रहेगी और इसे किसी भी तरह से रोका नही जा सकेगा। पर सजा मिलने से आइन्दा से कोई भी निर्माता-निर्देशक ऐसी फिल्म बनाने का साहस नहीं जुटाएगा और यदि वह साहस जुटाता भी है तो उसे अभिनेता नहीं मिल सकेंगे।
.
कृपया @pmoindia पर यह ट्वीट करें - @pmoindia I instruct you to order jury trial on padmavti movie makers. #JuryTrialOnPadmawatiMakers .
.
स्थायी समाधान सेंसर बोर्ड चेयरमेन पर राईट टू रिकॉल प्रक्रिया है। इस क़ानून के आने से ऐसी गलीज फिल्मो रिलीज करने वाले चेयरमेन को जनता नौकरी से निकाल सकेगी, और नया आने वाला चेयरमेन तमीज से काम करेगा। ज्यूरी सिस्टम प्रक्रियाएं लागू होने से इस तरह की फिल्में बनाने वाले और इन्हे प्रमोट करने वाले अपना प्रभाव खो देंगे।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Nov 09 2017 13:24:18 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Piyush Jain:

बहुत से कार्यकर्ता मात्र एक संपन्न और धनी एनजीओ खड़ा करने में रूचि रखते है और उसके बाद (या शायद कभी नहीं) देश की समस्याओं पर कुछ करने के लिए सोचते हैं !!
.
मैं बहुत से शिक्षावादी कार्यकर्ताओं (यानि जो कार्यकर्ता गरीब बच्चों में शिक्षा सुधार करने के लिए काम करते हैं ), गौसेवा कार्यकर्ताओं (भारत में गौरक्षा और गौशाला बनवाने के लिए काम करने वाले ), स्वास्थ्य कार्यकर्ताओं (जिन्हें भारत के सभी लोगों का स्वास्थ्य सुधारना है ), इत्यादि के संपर्क में आया हूँ .
.
और 90% से ऊपर वरिष्ठ शिक्षावादी कार्यकर्ताओं ने राईट टू रिकॉल जिला शिक्षा अधिकारी के कानूनी ड्राफ्ट का समर्थन अपनी ट्विटर और फेसबुक वाल पर करने से इनकार कर दिया है, और उन्होंने राईट टू रिकॉल राज्य और केंद्रीय शिक्षा मंत्री के कानूनी ड्राफ्ट को भी अपनी फेसबुक और ट्विटर वाल पर समर्थन करने से इनकार कर दिया है !! और उन्होंने अभी तक राज्य या भारत में शिक्षा सुधार के लिए किसी प्रकार का कानूनी ड्राफ्ट नहीं दिया है !!
.
और 90% से ऊपर गोसेवकों ने “गोहत्या के मामलों में नार्को-टेस्ट” या “गोहत्या के मामलों में जूरी सुनवाई और निर्णय” या “राज्य गाय कल्याण मंत्री के ऊपर राईट टू रिकॉल” इत्यादि कानूनी ड्राफ्ट का समर्थन अपनी फेसबुक और ट्विटर वाल करने से इनकार कर दिया है !! और उन्होंने अभी तक राज्य या भारत में गौहत्या रोकने के लिए किसी प्रकार का कानूनी ड्राफ्ट नहीं दिया है !!
.
और ठीक इसी प्रकार, 90% से ऊपर वरिष्ठ स्वास्थ्य कार्यकर्ताओं ने राईट टू रिकॉल खाद्य मिलावट नियंत्रण अधिकारी, राईट टू रिकॉल राज्य और केंद्रीय स्वास्थ्य मंत्री इत्यादि कानूनी ड्राफ्ट को समर्थन देने से इनकार कर दिया है !!
.
कुछ कनिष्ठ (जूनियर) कार्यकर्ताओं ने इन कानूनी ड्राफ्ट का समर्थन किया लेकिन जैसा बहुत से एनजीओ में होता है, एक जूनियर कार्यकर्ता को सीनियर कार्यकर्ता की पहले अनुमति लेना होती है !!
.
इसके निम्नलिखित कारण हो सकते हैं :
.
(1) एनजीओ मालिक को कानून बदलने या व्यवस्था परिवर्तन की जरुरत नहीं लगती या उसे लगता है कि कार्यकर्ता किसी कानून को बदल ही नहीं सकते
.
(2) एनजीओ के मालिक पहले एक बड़ा एनजीओ खड़ा करना चाहते है और फिर वे किसी समस्या पर राज्य / राष्ट्र स्तर पर काम करना चाहेंगे !! तो पहले वह अपने एनजीओ के पास कुछ करोड़ की संपत्ति या ऐसी कुछ करोड़ की 10 संपत्ति जमा करना चाहता है और फिर राज्य या राष्ट्र स्तर पर वह किसी समस्या पर काम करेगा
.
(3) और एक बार उसका ये करोड़ो की संपत्ति वाला एनजीओ खड़ा करने का उद्देश्य पूरा हो जाता है, वह केवल तब ही राष्ट्र हित के मुद्दे पर काम करने का फैसला करेगा, जब उसके एनजीओ के पास कुछ 100 करोड़ की संपत्ति जमा हो जाएगी और आगे ऐसे ही चलता रहेगा, अर्थात वह कभी भी राज्य या राष्ट्र की समस्या या मुद्दे पर काम करने के लिए तय नहीं करेगा !!
.
तो यहाँ बहुत से वाजिव या “वाजिव दिखने वाले” कारण हैं
.
तो मेरी जूनियर कार्यकर्ताओं से विनती है-- यदि आप भारत में व्यवस्था परिवर्तन के लिए अपनी उर्जा और अपना समय लगाना चाहते हैं , तो इसकी काफी संभावनाए है कि आपका सीनियर एनजीओ मालिक या पार्टी मालिक आपसे व्यवस्था परिवर्तन से पहले उसके एनजीओ या पार्टी के लिए करोड़ों की संपत्ति जमा करने के लिए कहेगा !! तो आपको ये तय करना होगा कि पहले आप कानून में बदलाव के लिए काम करना चाहते हैं या रुकना . मेरा सुझाव है -- उसके एनजीओ की संपत्ति करोड़ों तक जाए इसके लिए ना रुके, जितना जल्दी हो सके, कृपया कानूनी ड्राफ्ट्स का समर्थन अपनी ट्विटर और फेसबुक वाल पर करें और साथ में इन ड्राफ्ट्स के प्रचार के लिए चुनाव लड़ें, अखबार में विज्ञापन दे, पर्चे बाँटें आदि गतिविधियाँ करें
.
(एक अलग बात: राईट टू रिकॉल आन्दोलन में हम किसी कार्यकर्ता से दान देने या दान इकठ्ठा करने लिए नहीं कहते हैं, सिवाय अखबार विज्ञापन के लिए .
.
तो अखबार विज्ञापन में, सीनियर किसी भी तरह की भौतिक संपत्ति अपने नाम पर या अपने एनजीओ या अपनी पार्टी के नाम पर इकठ्ठा नहीं कर पायेगा. तो कोई भी कह सकता है- सीनियर कार्यकर्ता को कुछ प्रसिद्धि मिलेगी . ठीक है, वह प्रसिद्धि पा सकता है, लेकिन प्रमुख प्रसिद्धि कानूनी ड्राफ्ट को मिलेगी और सीनियर रेकालिस्ट को केवल अमुख्य प्रसिद्धि मिलेगी . लेकिन दुसरे एनजीओ से इसकी तुलना करें . यहाँ एनजीओ का मालिक दान को उस एनजीओ के नाम पर कुछ जमीन या भवन आदि में बदल देता है और प्रमुख प्रसिद्धि भी एनजीओ के मालिक को ही मिलती हैं !!
.
अन्य शब्दों में, बहुत से एनजीओ को मिलने वाला दान उनकी संपत्ति और एनजीओ मालिक की प्रसिद्धि में इजाफा करता है जबकि राईट टू रिकॉल के विज्ञापन में दिया गया आर्थिक सहयोग केवल कानूनी ड्राफ्ट की प्रसिद्धि बढ़ाता है और थोड़ी बहुत प्रसिद्धि सीनियर रिकालिस्ट को मिलती है और राईट टू रिकॉल पार्टी या सीनियर रिकालिस्ट के पास शून्य संपत्ति बढ़ती है . इसलिए ये एक अति महत्वपूर्ण अंतर है, अन्य एनजीओ /पार्टियों और राईट टू रिकॉल पार्टी के लिए दान देंने में या चंदा इकठ्ठा करने में ).
=============

Proposed law-draft #DigSignIndia
.
------------------------------------------
DigSignIndia - enable voter to put his public key, other details in voterlist
------------------------------------------
.
[ comment : the draft has 2 parts -
.
Part - I : Basic instructions for citizens
.
Part – II : Instructions for officers and comments ]
.
-----------------------------------
Part - I : Basic instructions for citizens
------------------------------------
.
(1) After this law is enacted, you (the voter) may (or need not) approach Clerk incharge of voter cards and provide him following information, in specified forms, and also mention whether you want the information to be kept confidential or make it public :
(1.a) your facebook id
(1.b) your twitterid
(1.c) your id on mygov.in
(1.d) your email id
(1.e) your phone number
(1.f) aadhar card number
(1.g) finger print
(1.h) sha1 hash of your digital signature’s public key
(1.i) other information as added by PM

(2) You (voter) can also inform whether you wish to make one or more of your above details public or keep one of more of the details confidential.
.
(3) First registration for each piece of information will be free, And thereafter, you will need to pay Rs 5/- per charge per information.
.
----------------------------------------
Part - II : Instructions for Officers and comments
----------------------------------------
.
(6) comment :The comments are not part of actual code of this legislation draft. The activists and voters may use comments to understand the draft. And Jurors may use comments as a reference to decide a case. But the comments are NOT binding on anyone. .
.
(7) After the law is enacted, DC will make arrangements so that Clerk at office of Talati or Tahsaildaar or Collector can enter the information mentioned in clause-1 into system. And if the voter wants information to be confidential, then it will not come on public website and will not come on voter-list given to public, election candidates etc. But if voter has requested that information about him be made public, then his information will be put on website and also given to public along with voter list
.
(8) comment : the voters and activists can see that using above information, they can directly communicate to any voter who has publicly disclosed his information such as facebook id, twitter-id etc. And one may be able to communicate via software and collect opinions of thousands / lakhs / crores of voters directly, with or without use of any central websites.
.
(9) comment to CM, Mayor, District / Tahsil / Gram Sarpanchs : you may pass law / resolution by making changes (eg replacing word PM by CM or Mayor etc and other necessary changes) and make this applicable at State / City / District / Sarpanch / Gram level. That way, you can create a True Digital State / City / District / Tahsil / Gram.
